# R153 — Every node, statement, scope and premise route

391 inherited nodes; no silently deleted IDs. All formulas are ordinary mathematical text. Open obligations are not closed by this export. Source originals and baseline are preserved.

## A:TOKEN_CRITERIA — Actual-token criteria

For a specified physical subhistory P over interval I, admission to P_actual requires actual nonempty event/state support, fidelity to induced physical relations, traceable causal continuity, representation of materially relevant boundary influences, traceable lineage, and invariance under invertible descriptions. These are domain requirements; no effective universally sufficient physical admission algorithm is supplied.

**Status:** DECLARED_CONTEXT_OR_METHOD (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. Pure symmetry/topology/quotient claims use their explicit abstract domains, not an unstated actual realization.

**Typed domain:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. Pure symmetry/topology/quotient claims use their explicit abstract domains, not an unstated actual realization.

**Source:** [A Appendix C](../../records/R128_Unified_Formal_Map_Audit_20261006/sources/A_UCT_I_v1_2.md); SHA256 `2e4469afbd1d1862463cc36396dd6e2b50c418f22d6f0fd584bbae33bfedc6f4`.

**Root role:** supplied definition, assumption, model, or source evidence; no derivation is asserted.

**Open obligations:** F153-09, F153-15, F153-16

## A:ACTUAL_TOKEN — Actual valid process token

P_actual denotes the declared domain of actual valid physical process occurrences satisfying TOKEN_CRITERIA at their specified intervals and boundaries. Quantification over P_actual is not quantification over all mathematical state tables or all coarse features.

**Status:** DECLARED_CONTEXT_OR_METHOD (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Admitted actual valid P, specified interval and boundary; A §2 actual-support/inherited-relations/continuity/accountability/traceability criteria. No claim that an arbitrary variable satisfies them.

**Typed domain:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. ## A:P3 — Persistence / cessation / embedding

For every P,Q and interval I in the declared physical comparison, Persist(P,I) AND Embed(P,Q,I) implies that P remains in P_actual throughout I. Persist requires continuing constitutive support/dynamics/boundary and lineage; embedding alone neither establishes persistence nor establishes cessation.

**Status:** DECLARED_CONTEXT_OR_METHOD (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. ## A:P6 — Token / type / lineage

Numerical token equality P=Q, complete-type equivalence D(P) isomorphic to D(Q), and directed causal-lineage relation P leads_to Q are distinct relations. Type equivalence need not imply token equality; lineage may branch or merge and is not an equivalence relation or a subject-count function.

**Status:** DECLARED_CONTEXT_OR_METHOD (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. ## A:D_ONTIC — Complete token-relative ontic organization

For each admitted occurrence P at the declared time/interval and comparison signature K, D_ontic_K(P) is the K-structure containing every distinction constitutive of that token. It need not contain every microfact in the substrate; a finite measured model is not presumed complete.

## A:K — Common structural signature K

Fix one many-sorted comparison signature K, including distinguished constitutive carrier, current-state pointing, time orientation, state/update/port/readout and constituent relations wherever claimed. Isomorphisms are sortwise bijections preserving and reflecting all K relations and commuting with its operations; only declared port symmetries are allowed. Physical and experiential structures use this SAME K.

## A:QSPACE — Structural-type quotient space

For the fixed admissible class of K-structures, S_K is its quotient by K-isomorphism and q_K(X) is X's class. Use a set-sized comparison universe, or state a universe convention, before using S_K as a set. P and Q can be distinct tokens with q_K(D(P))=q_K(D(Q)).

## A:GEOMETRY — Predeclared invariant topology / pseudometric

Fix before the target comparison a topology tau_K or a nonnegative symmetric triangle-satisfying pseudometric delta_K on S_K. No separation of distinct points by delta is required. Use the identical geometry for physical and experiential types; C1 supplies no unique or empirically calibrated choice.

**Status:** DECLARED_CONTEXT_OR_METHOD (inherited; not a machine proof).

## A:SUBJECT_FIREWALL — Experience-bearing process != canonical subject

The basal theory quantifies over P_actual without a required global exclusive subject selector. Using 'subject' for a process requires naming that process; using it for a human-like owner/conceptual self requires separate organization/semantic predicates. Neither use identifies every overlap or nested process with one canonical owner.

## A:COMPLETE_EQ — Complete current ontic equivalence

The two specified actual occurrences P,Q, with their compared present times and identical K, satisfy D_ontic_K(P) isomorphic to D_ontic_K(Q). Every currently constitutive consequence of any other claimed difference is included or shown screened.

**Status:** DECLARED_ASSUMPTION (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Same current complete token-relative ontic organization in the common signature, not merely equal observations.

**Typed domain:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. Pure symmetry/topology/quotient claims use their explicit abstract domains, not an unstated actual realization.

**Source:** [A Appendix C](../../records/R128_Unified_Formal_Map_Audit_20261006/sources/A_UCT_I_v1_2.md); SHA256 `2e4469afbd1d1862463cc36396dd6e2b50c418f22d6f0fd584bbae33bfedc6f4`.

**Root role:** supplied definition, assumption, model, or source evidence; no derivation is asserted.

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

## A:EQUIV_SELECTOR — Equivariant deterministic selector

S is a deterministic set-valued selector on the declared structure domain with S(gW)=gS(W) for every admitted isomorphism g; at an automorphism of W its output is invariant. A singleton selector is a special case; a random seed is extra structure.

**Status:** DECLARED_ASSUMPTION (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Deterministic selector S with S(gW)=gS(W); no extra labels or random seed.

**Typed domain:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. ## A:TOY_MATH — Three-register / four-setting mathematics

Over F2, for each alpha,beta in {0,1}, x=(s,a,b), u in {0,1}, T_u(x)=(s XOR u,s XOR alpha*b,s XOR beta*a), y=s. Fix one clock tick, all 27 coordinate clamps before update, buffered no-feedback s port, pointed state and named constituent slots. Compare mechanisms at fixed alpha,beta; for pair retention hold the same s boundary trajectory and vary all four initial (a,b), with no subsequent pair resets.

**Status:** STIPULATED_MODEL (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Typed actual occurrences at specified intervals/boundaries and common complete K for ontic claims; finite models/views and externally declared bridge targets remain separate sorts. ## B:BAC — Seven bridge admissibility conditions

BAC(B_T,D_T) is the conjunction of seven requirements: source anchoring; output blindness/non-oracularity; bridge-rule fixation across instances; no post-hoc target refitting; covariance under admitted reparameterizations; possible failure/undefinedness; explicit imported commitments and residuals. It is a methodological predicate requiring evidence, not a theorem from the translator syntax.

**Status:** METHOD_NOT_AUTOMATICALLY_SATISFIED (inherited; not a machine proof).

## D:FRECHET — Sharp decision interval from marginals

delta=max_J(|J|-|N(J)|); maximum completed tasks=n-delta. Every randomized legal schedule satisfies sum_i P(X_i=1)<=n-delta and min_(i in J)P(X_i=1)<=|N(J)|/|J| for nonempty J.

## R134:PROBE_DUAL — Exact weighted binary probe guarantee

Forced-probe robust success equals min_(0<=q<=1) F(q), F(q)=sum_y max(q*a_y,(1-q)*b_y). Evaluate endpoints and breakpoints q=b_y/(a_y+b_y) ONLY for y with a_y+b_y>0. Rows with both masses zero contribute identically zero and supply no breakpoint.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Two fixed hidden mechanisms, finite probe transcript, terminal actions correct only for their corresponding mechanism; no later useful signal. Weighted transcript masses include specified conditional opportunity survival. Mathematical task success, not phenomenology, physical sensor validation or generic acquisition utility. One fixed probe followed by one terminal choice; not a solution of unrestricted adaptive sensing.

**Typed domain:** Finite persistent model/state coordinates and one common randomized observable policy; private coins independent of nature, fixed finite horizon. Success-profile vectors precede minimax. Probe survival and transcript probabilities are joint fixed data; supports with zero mass remain harmless.

**Source:** [R134_Quantitative_Control.md §6](../../records/R134_Quantitative_Control_and_Probe_Cost_20261007/R134_Quantitative_Control.md); SHA256 `6222aec3a8b718c164d634098e3035a1f449e944d0ae40a222e6b12c67aefdc1`.

- `R133:PERSISTENT_MODEL`, `R134:PROBE_MODEL`, `R134:QUANT_MODEL`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r134_probe:** For fixed q optimize each decision independently to obtain max(q*a_y,(1-q)*b_y). Finite convex minimax gives the dual. This convex piecewise-affine function attains a minimum at an endpoint or a breakpoint with a_y+b_y>0; zero-zero rows vanish.

## R135:ACTUAL_PROFILE — Grounded capability map on complete actual types

Actual valid process tokens, a common complete structural signature and fixed grounded tasks/ports/resources/observation rules supply a well-defined guarantee-region map s->G(s) invariant under allowed structural isomorphisms.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_MODEL_OR_DOMAIN_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Actual support and complete-type adequacy are independent premises, not certified by finite profile examples, decoder scores or small transition error.

**Typed domain:** Nonempty compact convex profile sets in the same finite-dimensional [0,1]^n, common tasks/resources/observation protocol; finite controlled abstraction uses uniform joint rows, same total legal actions and a pulled-back payoff. Partial-legality countermodels have their own domain.

**Source:** [R135_Capability_Preservation.md §9](../../records/R135_Capability_Preservation_and_Abstraction_20261007/R135_Capability_Preservation.md); SHA256 `f5e1631e80de38093e88c45fea2006e80b344f35edfe0f347043478d4950e1a7`.

**Root role:** supplied definition, assumption, model, or source evidence; no derivation is asserted.

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

## R135:UCT_INTERPRETATION — Published identification criteria applied to capability guarantees

Under A:C1 and ACTUAL_PROFILE, different G implies different complete experiential types; G identifies full experiential type iff s->G(s) is injective; a selected coordinate is recoverable iff constant on G-fibers.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Direct application of published C:P1/P2, not a new constitutive theorem. Equal guarantees alone do not prove experiential equality; no universal noninjectivity on every actual domain asserted. No new U1 gate.

**Typed domain:** Nonempty compact convex profile sets in the same finite-dimensional [0,1]^n, common tasks/resources/observation protocol; finite controlled abstraction uses uniform joint rows, same total legal actions and a pulled-back payoff. Partial-legality countermodels have their own domain.

**Source:** [R135_Capability_Preservation.md §9](../../records/R135_Capability_Preservation_and_Abstraction_20261007/R135_Capability_Preservation.md); SHA256 `f5e1631e80de38093e88c45fea2006e80b344f35edfe0f347043478d4950e1a7`.

## R136:STATIC_SETUP — Fixed one-shot experiments and universal decision comparison

P_theta(x),Q_theta(y) are fixed experiments; V_P(u),V_Q(u) optimize a common finite decision problem after observation with full-support prior p. Translators R(x|y) are independent of theta.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_MODEL_OR_DOMAIN_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite hidden parameter and signal sets; fixed row-stochastic observation experiments P,Q; one terminal decision after observation; full-support fixed prior; all finite randomized decision rules; no omitted costs, deadlines or side effects. A statistical postprocessor is not a physical organization isomorphism.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

**Source:** [R136_Causal_Translation.md §2](../../records/R136_Causal_Translation_and_Query_Order_20261007/R136_Causal_Translation.md); SHA256 `1848a0833cda279b8b0f6727fc5c53d7a75c8a4bb02aeeb9b45a038c86048b22`.

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

## R136:BLACKWELL — Classical all-decision comparison admits one stochastic translator

P=QR for a common stochastic R iff V_Q(u)>=V_P(u) for every finite decision problem. It suffices to vary all utilities with action set X. Utilities can be normalized to [0,1].

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite hidden parameter and signal sets; fixed row-stochastic observation experiments P,Q; one terminal decision after observation; full-support fixed prior; all finite randomized decision rules; no omitted costs, deadlines or side effects. A statistical postprocessor is not a physical organization isomorphism. Classical Blackwell randomization theorem, not a new project theorem. Varying every utility is stronger than reweighting one fixed success-profile family.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

**Source:** [R136_Causal_Translation.md §2](../../records/R136_Causal_Translation_and_Query_Order_20261007/R136_Causal_Translation.md); SHA256 `1848a0833cda279b8b0f6727fc5c53d7a75c8a4bb02aeeb9b45a038c86048b22`.

- Route `r136_blackwell`: **all of** `R136:STATIC_SETUP`.

**Expanded terminal premises** (each line is one alternative conjunction; terminals are not all proven):

- `R136:STATIC_SETUP`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r136_blackwell:** S={QR} is compact convex. If P is outside, strict separation gives c; choosing actions X and utility c(theta,x)/p_theta makes the identity rule after P outperform every Q rule. Conversely concatenate R and any P decision.

## R136:STREAM_SETUP — Causal row kernels and uniform path simulation loss

Causal(Y,X) contains stochastic full-path kernels whose output-prefix marginal at time t depends only on y_<=t. d_c(P<-Q)=min_R max_theta TV(P_theta,Q_theta R) over that polytope.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_MODEL_OR_DOMAIN_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite exogenous path experiments P_theta,Q_theta with common parameter and clock; one causal translator sees only current/past Y and uses private randomness independent of theta and the entire stream. No action feedback into future observations. All output-prefix marginals independent of future inputs. Physical port adequacy is not established. One kernel must work for all parameters, including indistinguishable histories; full-alphabet constraints permit arbitrary causal extension of unused histories.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

**Source:** [R136_Causal_Translation.md §3](../../records/R136_Causal_Translation_and_Query_Order_20261007/R136_Causal_Translation.md); SHA256 `1848a0833cda279b8b0f6727fc5c53d7a75c8a4bb02aeeb9b45a038c86048b22`.

## R136:CAUSAL_LP — Finite causal translation is exactly a linear feasibility certificate

A common causal transducer exists iff R>=0, row sums one, prefix nonanticipation constraints and P=QR are jointly feasible. Minimum uniform TV error is attained and is a finite LP via absolute-value epigraph variables.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite exogenous path experiments P_theta,Q_theta with common parameter and clock; one causal translator sees only current/past Y and uses private randomness independent of theta and the entire stream. No action feedback into future observations. All output-prefix marginals independent of future inputs. Physical port adequacy is not established. Construct conditional time kernels by prefix probability ratios; define zero-denominator rows arbitrarily. This is not a certificate for arbitrary controlled feedback.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

**Source:** [R136_Causal_Translation.md §3](../../records/R136_Causal_Translation_and_Query_Order_20261007/R136_Causal_Translation.md); SHA256 `1848a0833cda279b8b0f6727fc5c53d7a75c8a4bb02aeeb9b45a038c86048b22`.

- `R136:STREAM_SETUP`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r136_causal:** A causal transducer has prefix marginals independent of future input. Conversely ratios of successive prefix marginals give normalized time kernels and telescope to R, with arbitrary zero-probability rows. TV uses linear absolute-value epigraphs.

## R136:COMPOSITION — Causal translators compose with additive uniform path error

d_c(P<-W)<=d_c(P<-Q)+d_c(Q<-W). A copied causal downstream decision rule loses at most d_c in any [0,1] payoff g_theta(translated_stream,action_path), with unchanged legal actions and no feedback.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite exogenous path experiments P_theta,Q_theta with common parameter and clock; one causal translator sees only current/past Y and uses private randomness independent of theta and the entire stream. No action feedback into future observations. All output-prefix marginals independent of future inputs. Physical port adequacy is not established. No additional raw-Y correlation in payoff; translator costs excluded unless modeled. Independent seeds implement composition; two-way simulation need not be a complete physical isomorphism.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

**Source:** [R136_Causal_Translation.md §4](../../records/R136_Causal_Translation_and_Query_Order_20261007/R136_Causal_Translation.md); SHA256 `1848a0833cda279b8b0f6727fc5c53d7a75c8a4bb02aeeb9b45a038c86048b22`.

- `R136:STREAM_SETUP`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r136_composition:** Sequentially compose the two causal implementations using independent seeds. Triangle inequality and TV contraction give the sum bound. Copying an admissible no-feedback decision kernel contracts TV again and bounds its [0,1] payoff.

## R136:QUERY_MODEL — Pre-query resource commitment with adaptive coordinate reads

For n>=2 and 0<=k<=n, read at most k coordinates of b in {0,1}^n before q in {1,...,n} is revealed; then guess b_q. V_nk=sup_policy min_(b,q) success.

## R136:TASK_ORDER — Perfect separate pre-announced tasks need not admit a delayed-task translator

For 1<=k<n every pre-announced q has full-reader and restricted-reader guarantee region [0,1]^(2^n). Under delayed q, full-to-restricted directional guarantee loss is (n-k)/(2n), reverse zero; no exact response translator respecting the restricted read budget and deadline exists.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS (inherited; not a machine proof).

This is a query-access budget, not arbitrary k-bit encoding of a fully seen string or quantum coding. Each comparison uses a common protocol internally; pre-announced and delayed protocols are explicitly different. This is not a counterexample to R135 or a universal impossibility of translators.

**Typed domain:** Finite static experiment or finite no-feedback stream with one parameter-independent causal kernel; preserve deadline, legal actions, randomization and query budget. Offline translation is a different admissible class.

## R145:SEPARATION — Active reachable realizers can share every descending-intervention law

FAMILY realizes z->z+u and is blind to k under the full common descending-intervention feedback class. All states are reachable in two updates and all updates bijective; every bit affects output and register updates mutually depend. Distinct k are nonisomorphic as labeled structures; zero vs nonzero also differ by a pointed self-loop invariant. One zero-input update followed by R_x(x,y)=(0,y) reveals k; R_x does not descend. Two uninterrupted updates satisfy T^k_v T^k_u(x,y)=(x+u,y+v), independent of k.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_PROJECT_EXTENSION_PRIORITY_UNVERIFIED (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Exactly FAMILY and TRANSPORT_SETUP. All finite-history laws, not all physical observation channels. Labeled pairwise nonisomorphism is not upgraded to unlabeled pairwise nonisomorphism. Reset needs one-tick access; two-tick endpoint statement excludes intermediate observations/interventions. No idle-state padding, but no universal minimality, integration measure or empirical consciousness claim.

**Typed domain:** Finite binary implementation family, fixed mechanism k, labeled ports and one-tick preparation/intervention/readout timing. Logical quotient versus represented implementation versus actual token are separate objects. Constitutive realization is a separate premise.

## R146:DEFINITION_CONTRACT — Common typed vocabulary for organization, experience, capability and self

Actual token, complete organization, model, view, estimate and type are distinguished. Experience retains C1 interpretive identity; basal phenomenal consciousness abbreviates nonempty experiential carrier. Intelligence is a well-defined isomorphism-invariant fixed-contract capability profile. Functional self targets are distinct from conceptual and phenomenal selfhood.

**Status:** SOURCE_ALIGNED_DEFINITIONS_AND_EXPLICIT_OPEN_BRIDGES (inherited; not a machine proof).

## R146:SELF_TARGET_SUFFICIENCY — Self-target information fibers and minimax error radius

Under SELF_CONTRACT, an abstract zero-error decoder exists iff Q(omega,a) is constant on each m fiber for every common plan a. Two same-fiber targets at TV distance delta force worst-case error >=delta/2. With unrestricted rows, optimal worst-case error equals the supremum over (m,a) of the smallest TV radius covering that fiber target set.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_CLASSICAL_APPLICATION (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite nonempty target alphabet, common legal plans, nonempty domain, exact target laws, arbitrary uncoupled decoder rows. No computability, resource, installation, statistical-estimation or semantic guarantee. Half-diameter is only a lower bound; three distinct point masses have radius 2/3 and diameter 1. Classical factorization/metric application of C:P2_COORD, not new consciousness mathematics.

**Typed domain:** Actual bearer and grounded attachment, selected self-target laws, installed model and separate abstract decoder class; conceptual Self_I and experiential mineness remain distinct targets. Finite symmetry/cell countermodels do not establish those semantic bridges.

**Source:** [records/R146_Experience_Intelligence_and_Self_20261007/Experience_Intelligence_and_Self_v0_1.md Proposition 2](../../records/R146_Experience_Intelligence_and_Self_20261007/Experience_Intelligence_and_Self_v0_1.md); SHA256 `0043d594b11d3e43c75cf023c03cbca5532d8dbf56a943546b6c3a2c6cc61692`.

**Open obligations:** F153-11, F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r146_self_target:** Apply fiber constancy to the law-valued map omega -> (Q(omega,a))_a. The triangle inequality yields delta/2. Unrestricted decoder rows optimize separately; each fiber radius attains its minimum on the finite probability simplex because the supremum-TV objective is Lipschitz. Three point masses have optimal uniform center radius 2/3.

## TA16:FINITE_CONTRACT — Finite causal diagnostic constructions

Finite nonnegative weighted digraphs n>=2 with cuts deleting all crossing edges; Boolean parity/shift maps; invertible recodings with transported interventions; odd repetition encoders E and majority decoders D with DE=id.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_DEFINITION_OR_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Each clause applies to its stated family, not every physical system.

**Typed domain:** Historical RRH-E is a separate conjectural package; finite graph/information/coding examples use the explicitly stated cuts, alphabets, intervention transport, modes and encoded states. Historical conjecture is not a new basal C1 axiom.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA16_v1_0.md Appendix G.1–G.4](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA16_v1_0.md); SHA256 `041a21d0a0863719188f591b2f51f94b591cc16a795d74ed6df047e058c48360`.

**Root role:** supplied definition, assumption, model, or source evidence; no derivation is asserted.

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

## TA16:CAUSAL_LIMITS — Connectivity, retention, common witness and redundancy limits

All-cut positive diagonal return iff strong connectivity (a witness walk length <=2(n-1)); strong connectivity alone does not determine retained information; incompatible modes cannot jointly witness different maxima; recoding does not change physics when interventions are transported; every finite Boolean F has an exact wrapper Ftilde=EFD on valid codewords.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_PUBLISHED_RESULT (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Nonnegative matrix clause excludes cancellation/single-vertex vacuity; capacity uses unrestricted deterministic input preparation.

**Typed domain:** Historical RRH-E is a separate conjectural package; finite graph/information/coding examples use the explicitly stated cuts, alphabets, intervention transport, modes and encoded states. Historical conjecture is not a new basal C1 axiom.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA16_v1_0.md Appendix G.1–G.4](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA16_v1_0.md); SHA256 `041a21d0a0863719188f591b2f51f94b591cc16a795d74ed6df047e058c48360`.

- Route `r149_ta16_causal_limits`: **all of** `TA16:FINITE_CONTRACT`.

**Expanded terminal premises** (each line is one alternative conjunction; terminals are not all proven):

- `TA16:FINITE_CONTRACT`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r149_ta16_causal_limits:** For strong connectivity concatenate paths crossing and returning through each cut. Conversely a source SCC admits no closed crossing walk. Parity broadcast on five bits retains one bit; invertible four-bit shift-XOR retains four. Identity and parity modes do not jointly witness full capacity and complete dependencies. Conjugate both dynamics and interventions. Finally Ftilde E=EF since DE=id; induction gives all horizons and majority corrects up to (n-1)/2 pre-update flips per block.

## TA17:FUNCTIONAL_CONTRACT — Current attribution and functional continuation objects

Keep M_attr, endogenous response quotient Z, intervention channel K, inheritance Gamma(S), successor-set belief mu, post-branch P_loc and motivational stake separate. M_attr is a nonexclusive current-source readout. Define Gamma(S)=I(U;Y_S)/H(U) in the joint interventional experiment p(u)p(y_S|do(U=u)), with finite variables and H(U)>0.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_DEFINITION_OR_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Fixed output/intervention family; current-process causal grounding is additional to MI. No numerical identity or phenomenality asserted.

**Typed domain:** Finite interventional variables with H(U)>0; internal attribution, functional quotient, inheritance, successor-set law and motivational stake are different objects. Transported recodings preserve experimental semantics; marginal posterior data are not a joint set law.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md §§4,12–14](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md); SHA256 `bec4944db161db725648a18962b8a6c069aacf7512a259483f9d81e646e36a78`.

## TA17:INHERITANCE — Scoped inheritance algebra and causal limits

Gamma is monotone in included outputs; bijective recoding preserves it when intervention distribution/semantics are transported. Readout MI is a lower bound under U->Y->O. Closed-lineage MI cannot increase without violating the specified Markov/no-side-channel premise. Shared-upstream MI alone does not establish current-process descent.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_PUBLISHED_RESULT (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Finite interventional variables with common H(U)>0; Markov clauses only under their stated chains.

**Typed domain:** Finite interventional variables with H(U)>0; internal attribution, functional quotient, inheritance, successor-set law and motivational stake are different objects. Transported recodings preserve experimental semantics; marginal posterior data are not a joint set law.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md Propositions 1–4,11; Theorem 1; Corollary 4](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md); SHA256 `bec4944db161db725648a18962b8a6c069aacf7512a259483f9d81e646e36a78`.

- `TA17:FUNCTIONAL_CONTRACT`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r149_ta17_inheritance:** Projection and data processing prove monotonicity/readout/closed-lineage claims. Bijective entropy and joint entropy invariance prove recoding invariance. Counterexample U fair, A=U and independently stored C=U yields I(U;C)=1 while intervening on A after separation leaves C unchanged.

## TA17:SET_LIMITS — Successor sets and nonadditive inheritance

Individual-only categorical labels cannot encode arbitrary successor sets. Under nonnegative membership, co-successor invariance and symmetry of equally full copies, unit-sum normalization fails on two full successors. Equal singleton inclusion marginals need not identify mu; equal singleton inheritance need not identify Gamma. Duplication violates conserved-mass and universal supermodularity; secret sharing violates universal submodularity.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_PUBLISHED_RESULT (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Existence counterexamples under FUNCTIONAL_CONTRACT; normalization includes nonnegativity and symmetry explicitly.

**Typed domain:** Finite interventional variables with H(U)>0; internal attribution, functional quotient, inheritance, successor-set law and motivational stake are different objects. Transported recodings preserve experimental semantics; marginal posterior data are not a joint set law.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md Propositions 5–10,13](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA17_v1_0.md); SHA256 `bec4944db161db725648a18962b8a6c069aacf7512a259483f9d81e646e36a78`.

- Route `r149_ta17_set_limits`: **all of** `TA17:FUNCTIONAL_CONTRACT`.

**Expanded terminal premises** (each line is one alternative conjunction; terminals are not all proven):

- `TA17:FUNCTIONAL_CONTRACT`

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r149_ta17_set_limits:** Use muE(10)=muE(01)=1/2 versus muJ(00)=muJ(11)=1/2. For U=(U1,U2), B=C=U1 and B=U1,C=U2 share singleton Gamma=1/2 but joint scores 1/2 vs1. For U,R independent fair, B=R,C=U XOR R gives singleton0,joint1. Full copies give singleton1+1,joint1. Cardinality and these values prove the remaining clauses.

## TA18:UNDERDETERMINATION — Existence of bridge-nonunique invariant models

Structural invariance requirements do not universally select a unique type-valued bridge: there is a two-physical-class/two-output model with identity scale maps satisfying them and permitting both a constant and a class-separating map.

## TA19:GROUP_SETUP — Physical candidate action and equivariant selector

A group G acts on physical configurations and their candidate fibers compatibly. At x0 let H be the stabilizer. A deterministic selector s obeys s(gx)=g s(x). For the orbit clause O is one transitive H-orbit and R is a nonempty H-invariant subset of O.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_DEFINITION_OR_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Physical symmetry must preserve the full admitted description; external marking changes H.

**Typed domain:** Compatible group actions and candidate fibers; separate finite-covering/unique-path-lifting and finite labeled-metric-landscape setups. Numerical supplement is an archived report. Main Markdown source coverage ends at section 5.

**Source:** [records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA19_v1_0.md §§3–4; records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA18_v1_0.md §7](../../records/R148_Publication_Census_20261007/sources/TA19_v1_0.md); SHA256 `53782a354e6dd1b037f8a6021d2a2bad150201cd086a5f6799757074dd21fc7e`.

## TA19:LOCAL — Local fixed-point and orbit constraints

s(x0) must be H-fixed; if no candidate is H-fixed no singleton selector exists. On a transitive orbit every nonempty invariant subset is the whole orbit. A finite transitive orbit has a unique invariant probability, the uniform law.

**Scope:** Exactly GROUP_SETUP; finite orbit required for the probability clause.

## TA19:TOPOLOGY — Fixed labels and locally finite continuous branches

P,Q are distinct fixed Hausdorff labels. Winners are Q for lambda<0, P for lambda>0 and {P,Q} at zero. Separately, a locally fixed finite candidate set has continuous z_P into metric spaces with capped within-label metric and cross-label distance 2.

**Status:** EXPLICIT_DEFINITION_OR_PREMISE (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** The two clauses have separate explicit premises; no candidate birth/death.

## TA19:REGULARITY — Probability obstruction, closed graph and landscape continuity

Exact-top-tier supported probability laws cannot be continuous at a strict two-sided winner switch. A closed-graph set completion must contain both side limits at the tie. The finite labeled landscape is Hausdorff-continuous when each branch is continuous.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_SUPPLEMENT_PROOFS_EXPLICIT (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Fixed labels are essential; no physical meaning assigned to the artificial cross-label distance.

**Typed domain:** Compatible group actions and candidate fibers; separate finite-covering/unique-path-lifting and finite labeled-metric-landscape setups. Numerical supplement is an archived report. Main Markdown source coverage ends at section 5.

**Expanded terminal premises** (each line is one alternative conjunction; terminals are not all proven):

- `TA19:TOPOLOGY`

**Open obligations:** F153-14, F153-15, F153-16

**Argument r149_ta19_regularity:** One-sided probability limits are delta_Q and delta_P and differ. Closed graph contains limits (0,Q),(0,P). For finite landscapes, Hausdorff distance is bounded by max_P min(1,d_P(z_P(x),z_P(x0))), which tends to zero.

## R149:COMPATIBILITY — Complete-target compatibility and the incompatible triple

Under C1-OI, e=h r iff ker(r) subset ker(k) iff k=a r; image-domain functions are unique. If r=b k, this holds iff b is injective on k(Omega). Thus full C1-OI, complete-experience sufficiency of r, and an equal-r/different-k witness cannot all hold.

**Status:** MANUAL_CONDITIONAL_PASS_PROJECT_SYNTHESIS (inherited; not a machine proof).

**Scope:** Same bearer/interval/complete type contract.

**Typed domain:** Nonempty configuration Omega, SAME actual bearer/time assignments and complete K, k/e type maps and arbitrary descriptor r on that domain. Optional type invariance r=bk must be supplied; selected f and complete e are distinct recovery targets.

**Source:** [records/R149_Nine_Source_Integration_20261007/COMPATIBILITY_AND_SELF_TARGETS.md §3](../../records/R149_Nine_Source_Integration_20261007/COMPATIBILITY_AND_SELF_TARGETS.md); SHA256 `07351da149b61cc08672c7e65d2ee45bb158e84847a0f66d96feba5753316279`.

**Open obligations:** F153-15, F153-16, F153-17

**Argument r149_r149_compatibility:** C1-OI gives ker(e)=ker(k). Apply the fiber criterion to e and then k. Define F(k(omega))=e(omega); it is well-defined and injective on the realized type image. Optional r=b k makes reflection exactly injectivity of b. A collapsed distinct-k witness contradicts complete e sufficiency.
